which posits no intrinsic cosmic goal; the human being is an evolved organism and the theory has no
built-in end-point. Aurobindo's is a metaphysical account whose engine is involved consciousness
pressing upward, whose direction is teleological, and whose terminus is the supramental or gnostic
being; the human being is a transitional rather than terminal type. He concedes that biological
mechanisms may describe surface processes, and offers instead an interpretation of why consciousness
appears and intensifies. The registers differ, so sufficiency in one does not refute the other.

Examining the claim, however, exposes what it presupposes. It assumes that questions about the
meaning and direction of emergence are legitimate and not disguised empirical questions; and it
assumes that the levels can be coordinated. The category-confusion objection presses here:
biological evolution, psychological growth and metaphysical manifestation are treated within one
narrative, and while Aurobindo distinguishes proximate mechanism from a philosophy of consciousness,
the transitions between levels require argument and cannot be inferred from biological data.

There is also a cost to the insulation. The same separation of registers that protects Aurobindo
from empirical refutation exposes him to the charge of unfalsifiability, since no biological finding
could count against him.

The verdict is therefore calibrated: supplementation is the right description of the relation, but
it purchases immunity from refutation at the price of immunity from confirmation.

#### Original 4 — 15 marks

**Discuss Sri Aurobindo's treatment of ignorance as divided consciousness, and show how it shapes
his account of liberation.** *(Answer in about 250 words.)*

**Model answer (255 words).**

For Aurobindo, ignorance is not sheer non-being or the absence of consciousness. It is consciousness
**narrowed, self-limited and divided** from its larger truth. The definition is positive, and
everything in his soteriology follows from that.

Its genealogy runs through the gradation of planes. Integral truth is native to the Supermind; the
Overmind distributes one truth into multiple powers; separative mind then treats partial standpoints
as self-sufficient wholes. Ignorance is thus generated by the system's own descent rather than
introduced from outside it.

Its lived form is the ego. The ordinary ego knows itself as an isolated individual because it has
lost awareness of its transcendent source, of cosmic unity, of its subliminal depths and of temporal
continuity. What remains after those four losses, taken as a centre, *is* the ego — which is why the
ego is not a thing that has limitations but the set of limitations mistaken for a self. Inconscience
is the extreme term of this concealment; mental ignorance is its active mode in thought, desire and
action.

The consequence for liberation is decisive. If ignorance were a privation, addition would cure it
and liberation would be informational. Because it is a narrowing of an intact consciousness,
liberation must be a **re-widening**: reintegration into the larger consciousness from which the
narrowing was made. Ignorance is accordingly real in its evolutionary effects but is not an
independent anti-divine substance, and is therefore removable in principle.

The explanatory cost is that "concealed consciousness" resists disproof; Aurobindo answers by graded
experience, but public verification remains incomplete.

#### Original 5 — 20 marks

**"Aurobindo's originality lies less in what he affirms than in what he refuses to deny."
Critically examine this assessment of his integral philosophy.** *(Answer in about 350 words.)*

**Model answer (346 words).**

The assessment is largely just, and testing it clarifies both the strength and the exposure of
Aurobindo's system.

What he affirms is, taken singly, inherited. Existence–Consciousness–Bliss as ultimate reality is
Upaniṣadic. The reality of individual centres is Sāṃkhya-Yogic. The critique of the ego as a surface
construction is shared with Buddhism. The insistence that spiritual life includes action draws on
the Gītā. Even the language of truth-bearing consciousness has Vedic ancestry.

What is distinctive is the pattern of refusals. He refuses the illusionist's denial of the world's
reality, and pays for it with the Supermind, which explains how the One becomes Many without
fragmentation and without illusion. He refuses the materialist's denial of Spirit, and pays for it
with involution and with consciousness-force, since only a consciousness that is also force can
conceal and recover itself. He refuses Patañjali's denial that nature can be transformed, and pays
for it with the double movement of ascent and descent. He refuses the Buddhist denial of any abiding
individual, and pays with the fourfold analysis of Self, individual eternal self, psychic being and
ego. And he refuses to let liberation terminate the spiritual life, paying with the triple
transformation and the ideal of divine life on earth.

The assessment therefore identifies a real structure: the doctrines are not free-standing
speculations but the price of specific refusals, which is why the system holds together.

Critically, two qualifications are needed. First, the refusals are not equally well grounded. The
refusal of illusionism is supported by the argument that a merely apparent world makes the direction
of evolution pointless; the refusal of materialism rests on involution, which is load-bearing and
close to question-begging against an emergentist who denies directionality. Second, negative
originality has a cost of its own: a philosophy built to preserve every affirmation risks
elaborateness, and the scheme of planes, beings and transformations depends substantially on yogic
experience for its warrant.

The verdict is that the assessment is accurate as description and incomplete as praise: Aurobindo's
refusals generate his originality, and also locate exactly where his system is most exposed.

#### Original 6 — 20 marks

**Evaluate the claim that Sri Aurobindo's philosophy answers the charge that Indian thought is
world-negating, and identify what remains unresolved.** *(Answer in about 350 words.)*

**Model answer (343 words).**

The claim is well founded at the level of doctrine and only partly settled at the level of
verification.

Aurobindo's answer operates on four fronts. **Ontologically**, the world is a real
self-manifestation of Existence–Consciousness–Bliss rather than a false appearance; multiplicity has
truth within unity, and the Absolute is dynamic because consciousness (**cit**) is inseparable from
consciousness-force (**cit-śakti**). **Cosmologically**, involution and evolution give the
world-process a point: Spirit conceals itself in Matter and recovers itself through Life and Mind,
so the long drama has a direction rather than being a detour from reality.
**Soteriologically**, liberation is necessary but preliminary; the task is the divinisation of
nature, and Integral Yoga accordingly seeks the transformation of mind, life **and body** by the
double movement of ascent and descent. **Teleologically**, the terminus is the gnostic being and
divine life on earth, not escape into another realm.

Set against the standard indictment, this is a substantial answer. The charge of world-negation
rests on the claim that Indian systems treat embodiment as an obstacle and release as the whole of
the spiritual task. Aurobindo denies both, and he does so from inside the non-dual tradition rather
than by abandoning it — which is why his response has more force than a merely modernising one.

Three matters remain unresolved. First, the **premise**: involution carries the world-affirmation,
and an emergentist can accept every biological fact and refuse the inference, so the affirmation is
secured within the system rather than against all comers. Second, the **evidence**: supramental
descent and the gnostic being are not publicly verifiable, and a doctrine whose delays can always be
explained as incomplete preparation risks insulation from criticism. Third, the **moral cost**: a
real world makes suffering real, and although evil is located in a stage of manifestation and
answered by transformation rather than escape, the cost of the cosmic process still falls on
individuals who did not choose it.

The evaluation is therefore that Aurobindo answers the charge more completely than any other modern
Indian system, and that what remains open is not his world-affirmation but its warrant.

**Examiner note.** In a 20-marker of this type, the "what remains unresolved" section should be
structurally signposted, as here, so that the examiner can see the evaluative half at a glance.

## OPTIONAL ADVANCED DEPTH — NOT REQUIRED FOR A CORE ANSWER

### Prerequisite before this block is usable

Everything required for a complete 10-, 15- or 20-mark answer on **all eight** owned previous-year
questions is already in the Basic block. Use this block only after the owner-file spine —
Existence–Consciousness–Bliss, Supermind, involution, evolution, psychic being, triple
transformation, Integral Yoga, ascent and descent, the gnostic being and the Life Divine — is secure
and after a clean attempt at the relevant question.

**Deployment rule.** In a 15- or 20-marker, **one** optional insight is usually enough and **two**
is the ceiling. Optional depth belongs in the final evaluative paragraph, normally to mark Aurobindo
off from Śaṃkara, Darwin or classical Yoga with more precision than the core contrast allows. No
core answer in this package depends on anything below.

### ADVANCED MODULE 1 — Is the Overmind a lower intensity or a different principle?

**The precise issue.** Is the Overmind merely a weaker degree of supramental consciousness, or is it
decisively different in kind because it **disperses truth into powers** rather than holding
indivisible unity?

**The rival readings.** Loose popular expositions blur the two, treating the Overmind as
"almost-Supermind". The stricter Aurobindonian reading sharply distinguishes supramental **integral
truth** from overmental **plurality**, and the Basic block follows that stricter reading.

**Objection and counter-reply.** *Objection:* the ladder becomes unverifiable metaphysical layering
— a taxonomy of experiences dressed as ontology. *Reply:* Aurobindo presents it as an explanatory
structure for unity-in-multiplicity and as spiritual phenomenology, not as a laboratory taxonomy;
its warrant is the work it does in explaining how ignorance becomes possible without being created.

**Source trail.** Sri Aurobindo, *The Life Divine*; *Letters on Yoga*; Haridas Chaudhuri, *The
Philosophy of Integralism*.

**When it is worth one line.** In a 2019- or 2023-type Supermind answer, after the core contrast, to
show that the distinction is structural rather than gradual.

### ADVANCED MODULE 2 — Psychic being against individual eternal self and classical witness

**The precise issue.** Is the psychic being the same as the classical soul or as witness-consciousness?

**The rival readings.** The owner-safe position is that it is an **evolving soul-principle**. The
rival confusion identifies it either with the Sāṃkhya **puruṣa**, a static witness, or with a fixed
Self (**ātman**) — either of which would make an evolving soul contradict an immutable one.

**Objection and counter-reply.** *Objection:* multiplying inner selves obscures rather than explains.
*Reply:* Aurobindo distinguishes surface ego, psychic being and transcendent Self precisely in order
to map different layers of growth and transformation, and the functions cannot be discharged by one
term without contradiction.

**Source trail.** Sri Aurobindo, *The Synthesis of Yoga*; *Letters on Yoga*; Chatterjee and Datta for
the classical background against which the contrast is drawn.

**When it is worth one line.** In a 2021-type psychic-being answer, in the examination half, to
convert the entity-multiplication objection into a considered reply rather than an unanswered
charge.

### ADVANCED MODULE 3 — Teleological evolution: interpretation or alternative science?

**The precise issue.** Is Aurobindo offering a **philosophical interpretation** of evolution, or an
**alternative science**?

**The rival readings.** The sympathetic reading treats his account as a metaphysical deepening of
evolution — an answer to a question biology does not ask. The critical reading treats it as
speculative spiritual teleology without scientific testability.

**Objection and counter-reply.** *Objection:* supramental evolution is not empirically demonstrable.
*Reply:* the claim is not meant as a substitute for biology but as a metaphysical account of why
consciousness-development is intelligible at all. The residual difficulty, already conceded in the
Basic block, is that the same separation of registers that protects the claim also insulates it.

**Source trail.** Sri Aurobindo, *The Life Divine*; S. Radhakrishnan, *Indian Philosophy* Vol. II;
Haridas Chaudhuri.

**When it is worth one line.** In any Darwin comparison at 15 or 20 marks, to name the disagreement
as a dispute about explanatory registers rather than about data.

### ADVANCED MODULE 4 — Liberation or collective transformation: which is central?

**The precise issue.** Is Aurobindo still primarily a **liberation** philosopher, or does he
**displace** liberation (**mokṣa**) with terrestrial transformation?

**The rival readings.** The conservative reading holds that liberation remains central and
transformation is its extension. The stronger integral reading holds that liberation is **preliminary**
to the transformation of life and collectivity — which is the reading the Basic block adopts, on the
strength of the printed doctrine that release is a foundation rather than a terminus.

**Objection and counter-reply.** *Objection:* collective supramental transformation looks utopian.
*Reply:* possibility and inevitability are cosmic **tendencies**, not guarantees of quick historical
arrival; there is no timetable, no political blueprint and no guaranteed species-event.

**Source trail.** Sri Aurobindo, *The Life Divine*; *The Synthesis of Yoga*; *Letters on Yoga*, with
*The Human Cycle* and *The Ideal of Human Unity* bounding the collective horizon only.

**When it is worth one line.** In a 2020- or 2022-type answer, to show that the balance between
liberation and transformation is itself an interpretive question rather than a settled reading.

### What not to over-study

Long plane-by-plane catalogues, Ashram history and every later interpreter's private vocabulary add
nothing once the Supermind–evolution–Integral Yoga spine is secure. The corpus rewards the spine and
the traps, not the inventory.

### When this block would stop being optional

Promotion would be triggered if a future paper directly asked about the Overmind as such, about the
psychic being **versus** the Self, about Aurobindo's relation to Darwin or to science as the
principal demand, or about whether transformation outruns liberation. Until then, these modules
remain enrichment.

### Three genuinely contested matters to report as contested

1. Whether Aurobindo's readings of the Veda, the Upaniṣads and the Gītā are **exegesis** or
   **philosophical reconstruction**. His defenders argue that he uses tradition creatively to express
   a yogic vision rather than antiquarian exegesis; the question remains open and should be reported
   as open.
2. Whether his system is best classified as **philosophy**, **yogic phenomenology**, **metaphysical
   theology**, or a synthesis of all three.
3. The status of the claims about **bodily transformation** and the supramental future, which are
   doctrinal commitments within the system rather than independently established results.

## CONSOLIDATED REGISTER NOTES

> Compressed revision notes for the complete topic, in the teaching order of the Basic block. The
> Optional Advanced material is marked separately in part **V** and is never required for a core
> answer.

### A. Placement, sources and the two denials

- Twentieth-century modern Indian philosopher; final thinker in Paper I Section B; **syllabus item
  20**, printed verbatim: `Aurobindo: Evolution, Involution; Integral Yoga.`
- Position: **integral non-dualism** — Vedāntic ontology, world-affirming cosmology, evolutionary
  soteriology.
- Refuses **three denials**: the Buddhist and the illusionist (the two *spiritual* denials of the
  world) and the materialist (the *modern* denial of Spirit).
- Source map: *The Life Divine* = metaphysics · *The Synthesis of Yoga* = method · *Letters on Yoga*
  = plane and psychic-being distinctions · *Essays on the Gita* = spiritual action · *The Human
  Cycle* / *The Ideal of Human Unity* = collective horizon only · *Savitri* / *The Mother* =
  corroborative, never sole proof.
- Chatterjee and Datta supply no substantive Aurobindo chapter; C. D. Sharma gives a compact survey.
- Corpus: **8 primary-owned parts of 112**, one in **every** year 2018–2025.
- Biography and Ashram history are context; the metaphysical sequence is the marks.

### B. The dynamic Absolute and the refusal of illusionism

- Ultimate reality = **Existence–Consciousness–Bliss (saccidānanda**, also written **sat-cit-ānanda)**:
  infinite, absolute, self-existent, conscious, blissful.
- Accepts **Brahman**; rejects **illusionism (māyāvāda)** as the final truth about the world.
- The Absolute is **dynamic, creative, self-manifesting**; the world is **real self-manifestation**,
  not false appearance.
- Matter, life and mind are **graded modes** of progressive divine manifestation ⇒ creation is
  meaningful and purposive.
- Reductio against illusionism: a merely illusory world makes the drama of evolution metaphysically
  pointless.
- Anti-illusionism: multiplicity has truth **within** unity, not against it; diversity is
  self-articulation, not a fall.
- Safe line: **transcendence does not cancel immanence.** The Divine is not exhausted by the world.
- Presupposes: a rich enough Absolute · consciousness ontologically basic · finite from Infinite
  without rupture or illusion.

### C. Consciousness as fundamental — consciousness-force

- **Cit** is inseparable from **cit-śakti**: consciousness is also **force**.
- Consciousness is **ontologically basic**; matter is its densest self-concealment; the Inconscient
  is self-oblivion, **not** absence.
- A purely static Absolute could neither involve nor evolve ⇒ Aurobindo's objection to a strictly
  qualityless reading of Advaita.
- Reverses the materialist arrow ⇒ sustained **critique of reductionism**, not of biology.
- **cit-śakti** is a power *of* the Divine, never a second substance (that would restore dualism).
- Buys directionality; costs independence — an emergentist accepts the same data and denies the
  inference.
- Trap: consciousness ≠ mind; "matter is self-concealed consciousness" ≠ "stones think".

### D. The Supermind — Truth-Consciousness and Real-Idea

- **Supermind (vijñāna)** = **Truth-Consciousness**; knows by **identity**; is the **Real-Idea**, in
  which knowledge and creative force are one act.
- Position: **between** Existence–Consciousness–Bliss and the lower creation; the **first creative
  determinative poise** of the Absolute.
- Solves the One→Many problem: without a mediating principle the transition is either **illusion** or
  an **unexplained leap**.
- Mind is disqualified as the mediator because mind already operates **by division**.
- Unity-in-multiplicity: each being seen **in its truth**, distinct but never absolutely separate;
  the whole in the part.
- Mind vs Supermind: indirect/divisive vs direct/integral · error-prone vs truth-native · abstract
  representation vs Real-Idea · lower plane vs supramental principle.
- Five-step argument: One and infinite → many and finite → mediator needed → mind cannot → therefore
  Supermind.
- Asked **2019 (15, "unique position")** and **2023 (10, "role in evolution")** — different emphases.
- Trap: not a superior intellect; not the Absolute; not the Overmind.

### E. The three poises

- **Comprehending / transcendent:** all things held in undivided unity; the many present but not
  stationed as separate centres.
- **Apprehending / creative:** a subject–object distinction projected **within** unity; the Divine as
  knower puts forth the cosmos as known — the poise of the cosmic Self or the **Lord (īśvara)**.
- **Projecting / individualising:** consciousness stands **in** each centre and knows from that
  standpoint; individuality is **real**.
- **Separateness begins only below the Supermind**, in Overmind and Mind — not at the third poise.
- Payoff: the One becomes many **without unreal illusion and without real fragmentation**;
  multiplicity is a **real self-modification** of consciousness.
- Report the naming caution: "comprehending / apprehending / projecting" is one standard
  presentation; defend the substance, not the labels.

### F. The gradation of planes; Overmind against Supermind

- Descent: Existence–Consciousness–Bliss → Supermind → Overmind → Intuitive Mind → Illumined Mind →
  Higher Mind → Mind → Life → Matter → Inconscient.
- Ascent (the yoga ladder): Higher Mind → Illumined Mind → **Intuition** → Overmind → Supermind.
- **Overmind** = plane of cosmic gods and vast powers; unity **first breaks** into independently
  self-existent powers; **last plane before ignorance becomes possible**.
- **Supermind** alone = indivisible truth-consciousness.
- Consequence: earlier attainments may reach liberation or overmental universality but **not** the
  supramental transformation of terrestrial nature.
- Existence–Consciousness–Bliss is **ultimate reality**, not one finite plane among planes.
- Matter is not anti-spiritual; the Inconscient is hidden, not absent.

### G. Knowledge by identity and knowledge by separation

- Grade 1 **knowledge by identity** — knower and known are one; native to the Absolute and the
  Supermind.
- Grade 2 **intimate direct contact** — separation present but bridged; higher spiritual/intuitive
  contact.
- Grade 3 **separative direct contact** — direct but external; sense-perception.
- Grade 4 **wholly separative indirect knowledge** — signs, symbols, images, inference, concepts;
  represents but does not possess. **This is ordinary human knowing.**
- Caution: the enumeration and naming vary; present it as a **graded scheme**, not a canonical list.
- Ignorance = wrong **mode**, not missing content ⇒ remedy is a **descent** that changes how we know,
  not an ascent to better concepts.
- Identity-knowing is **determinate and comprehensive**, not blank ineffability: it knows the many
  *as* the One.
- Objections: identity collapses knowledge into being (reply: **cit-śakti**; parallel with Advaita's
  self-luminosity, **svayaṃ-prakāśa**) · unverifiable (reply: repeatable inner discipline; residue:
  verification relocated) · metaphorical vocabulary (residue: hard to translate into analytic
  criteria).

### H. Involution and the Inconscient

- **Involution** = the Spirit's self-concealment in progressively denser forms; **logically prior**
  to evolution.
- Principle: **nothing evolves out of Matter unless its power was already involved in it.**
- Descent runs the full plane series; concealment is maximal at Matter; the **Inconscient** is the
  nadir — consciousness hidden, not absolutely absent.
- Matter = **Spirit in self-oblivion**, holding the whole divine potential latent ⇒ transformable,
  not merely escapable.
- **Ontological, not chronological** — no dated cosmic "fall".
- Against materialism: matter basic vs last term of self-concealed Spirit; consciousness produced vs
  latent; teleology absent vs built in; mechanical emergence vs unveiling.
- Against Sāṃkhya: not an independent unconscious **prakṛti**. Against Advaita: not an illusory
  appearance to be sublated.
- Weak point to concede: load-bearing and close to **question-begging** against an emergentist.

### I. Ignorance as divided consciousness

- Ignorance = consciousness **narrowed, self-limited, divided** from its larger truth — **not**
  absence.
- Genealogy: integral truth (Supermind) → distributed powers (Overmind) → partial standpoints taken
  as self-sufficient wholes (mind).
- The ego = **four simultaneous losses**: transcendent source · cosmic unity · subliminal depths ·
  temporal continuity.
- **Inconscience** = extreme term of concealment; **mental ignorance** = its active mode in thought,
  desire and action.
- Status: real in evolutionary effects, **not** an independent anti-divine substance ⇒ a restricted
  operation **capable of reintegration**.
- Cost: "concealed consciousness" resists disproof; concede once.

### J. Evolution — teleology, transitional man, involved possibility

- **Evolution** = progressive re-emergence of the Spirit involved in Matter; the reverse disclosure
  of involution.
- Line: **Matter → Life → Mind → Supermind**; mind is **not final**.
- Motive force: the involved Divine **pressing to manifest**; the process is **spiritual and
  teleological**.
- **Man is a transitional being**; mind is intermediate, not terminal; humanity bridges animal
  mentality and supramental consciousness ⇒ history is unfinished.
- Nature evolves **unconsciously** up to man; in man evolution can become **conscious** ⇒ yoga
  accelerates the hidden evolutionary intention.
- Involved possibility: what is involved is the **power or principle** of consciousness, **not**
  miniature preformed objects ⇒ continuity of potential **with** novelty of form.
- Four dimensions: transcendent Divine · cosmic consciousness-force · individual evolution ·
  collective transformation.
- Cosmic direction ≠ automatic individual attainment; no timetable, blueprint or guaranteed
  species-event.

### K. Spiritual evolution against biological reduction

- Darwin: empirical register · variation and natural selection · no intrinsic goal · evolved organism
  · no built-in end-point.
- Aurobindo: metaphysical register · involved consciousness pressing upward · teleological ·
  transitional being · supramental terminus.
- Safe line: **supplements** biology, does not refute it; biological mechanisms may describe surface
  processes.
- Objections to concede: **category confusion** (transitions between levels need argument) and
  **unfalsifiability** (insulation from every challenge).
- Reversal against materialism: matter is a derivative poise of concealed consciousness.

### L. The fourfold individual

| Term | Evolves? | Function |
|---|---|---|
| **Self (ātman)** | No | eternal, unborn, immutable Spirit; never bound or altered |
| **Individual eternal self (jīvātman)** | No | presides over the whole line of births without descending into change |
| **Psychic being (caitya puruṣa)** | **Yes** | evolving soul-personality around the divine spark; gathers the essence of experience; comes forward in **psychicisation** |
| **Ego / surface personality** | changes, but is not the true being | provisional knot of mind-life-body organising practical identity |

- Psychic being = **delegate of the individual eternal self in the evolution**.
- Inner **puruṣa** and **prakṛti** are redeployed **psychologically** — not accepted as ultimate
  dualism.
- Payoff: reconciles Advaitic freedom of the Self, real individual centres, and genuine growth.
- Objection: entity-multiplication. Reply: distinct functions. Residue: stipulative without yogic
  psychology.
- Traps: psychic being ≠ Sāṃkhya **puruṣa** · psychic being ≠ individual eternal self · the ego is
  provisional, not unreal.

### M. The triple transformation

- **Psychic** — the psychic being awakens and comes forward; the being is reoriented **from ego to
  soul**. *Changes the centre.*
- **Spiritual** — Higher Mind, Illumined Mind, Intuition and Overmind descend and spiritualise mind,
  life and bodily consciousness; peace, light, wideness, universality, detachment from ego. *Changes
  the contents.*
- **Supramental** — the **Supermind itself** descends; mind, life **and body** become instruments of
  truth-consciousness; the **gnostic being** appears. *Changes the nature.*
- Order argued, not stipulated: (i) higher influx is safely assimilated only on a soul-centred base;
  (ii) only indivisible truth can undo division.
- Not printed in the syllabus clause, yet directly demanded in **2018** and **2022**.
- Distinct from Integral Yoga: transformation is the **process**, yoga is the **participation**.

### N. Integral Yoga — the three integrations and the method

- **Integral Yoga (pūrṇa-yoga)** = the conscious means of accelerating evolution and enabling
  supramental transformation.
- Three integrations: (a) of **paths** — knowledge (**jñāna**), devotion (**bhakti**), works
  (**karma**); (b) of the **being** — mind, life and body; (c) of **individual and collective
  destiny** — divine life on earth.
- Method: **aspiration** (opens the path) · **rejection** (removes resistance) · **surrender
  (samarpaṇa)** to the Divine Mother / **Śakti** (allows the higher force to act).
- Not merely moral disciplines but psychological-spiritual **conditions** for transformation.
- The **Divine Mother / Śakti** is central; surrender is **conscious offering**, not passivity; the
  supramental descent is **mediated** by this Power.
- **Liberation is a beginning, not an end**; release is a foundation, the task is divinisation of
  nature.
- "**All life is Yoga**": work, relation, struggle, body, society and history are fields of practice.
- Not the eight-limbed discipline (**aṣṭāṅga-yoga**) renamed: renunciation of life as the final ideal
  is explicitly rejected.

### O. The double movement of ascent and descent

- **Ascent:** ordinary mentality → Higher Mind → Illumined Mind → Intuition → Overmind → Supermind;
  frees consciousness from ego-bound mentality.
- **Descent:** peace, light, force, wideness and finally supramental truth into **mind, life and
  body**.
- Failure modes: **ascent alone** may liberate yet leave the lower nature untransformed; **descent
  alone** is impossible without prior contact.
- Recurs through all three transformations; the practitioner prepares, the Divine **Śakti** mediates.
- Method-level signature of the metaphysics: escape needs only ascent, transformation needs the
  return.
- Verified **2024** wording: “Our Yoga is a double movement of ascent and descent.”

### P. Against world-negating liberation — Patañjali and Advaita

- **Patañjali:** Sāṃkhya–Yoga dualism · **kaivalya** (isolation of **puruṣa** from **prakṛti**) ·
  **citta-vṛtti-nirodha** and the eight limbs · withdrawal · cessation and separation.
- **Aurobindo:** dynamic non-dualism · transformation of **prakṛti** · aspiration-rejection-surrender
  · divinisation · truth-conscious participation.
- Compact contrast: release **from** prakṛti versus transformation **of** prakṛti.
- **Ṛtambharā caution — echo, not identity.** Truth-bearing insight (**ṛtambharā prajñā**) is a
  purified **citta-vṛtti** arising with **nirvicāra-vaiśāradya** and leading toward seedless
  absorption (**nirbīja samādhi**); it **must itself cease** for **kaivalya**. Supramental knowledge
  is not a mental modification at all but a plane to be **established in life**. Never write that
  Aurobindo adopts Patañjali's **ṛtambharā**.
- **Advaita:** world illusory vs real manifestation · knowledge dissolving appearance vs
  transformation of earthly life · realisation vs realisation **plus** divinisation · multiplicity in
  ignorance vs in the Supermind's unity-in-multiplicity.
- Asymmetry worth stating: with Advaita he **shares** the ontology and differs on cosmology; with
  Patañjali he differs on **both**.

### Q. The gnostic being, the Life Divine and the two partial negations

- 2025 statement: the operative word is **negation**. Ascetic affirms Spirit, denies Matter;
  materialist affirms Matter, denies Spirit; each **absolutises one pole** and rejects the other.
- Integral affirmation: **Spirit is the truth of Matter; Matter is the field of Spirit's
  manifestation.** Licensed by involution and evolution respectively.
- **Gnostic being:** knowledge direct and integral · action from truth-consciousness, not ignorance,
  conflict or ego · the division between inner and outer, individual and cosmic, healed in lived
  consciousness · body and life as **instruments, not obstacles**.
- **Life Divine** = divinised existence **on earth**. It is **not** post-mortem heaven, **not**
  private mystical release, **not** political utopia, **not** the perfection of ordinary intellect.
- Aim: neither world-flight nor flat naturalism.

### R. Divine life as possibility and inevitability

- **Possible** because: (i) Matter is involved Spirit; (ii) life and mind have **already** emerged
  from apparently inconscient Matter — the one premise the opponent concedes; (iii) the Supermind
  coordinates unity and multiplicity and is not foreign to manifestation; (iv) psychic and spiritual
  transformation prepare the instrument; (v) the gnostic being names the individual result.
- **Inevitable** only as the **inner direction** of cosmic evolution: mind is transitional, involved
  truth-consciousness presses toward manifestation.
- It does **not** mean universal individual attainment, a fixed historical timetable, or a mechanical
  effect of effort.
- Requires **both** an aspiration/call from below **and** a sanction/descent from above.
- Calibration: **stronger than mere possibility, weaker than empirical prediction with a specified
  date and mechanism.**
- Collective transformation = the spread of a new principle of consciousness, **not** rule by a
  self-certified spiritual elite; *The Human Cycle* and *The Ideal of Human Unity* bound the horizon.
- Status: speculative; defended by coherence, prior emergence and disciplined inner evidence.

### S. The problem of evil

- Sharper for Aurobindo: a **real** world blocks the Advaitic sublation route; suffering rooted in the
  **Inconscient precedes moral choice**, blocking the free-will defence.
- Chain: involution → **Inconscience** → extreme division → ego, desire, conflict, error, pain.
- Evil is **real in manifestation but not original** in the Divine; **real but not ultimate**;
  **instrumental and transitional** (the Inconscient's resistance makes the drama of emergence
  possible).
- Answer: **transformation** by supramental descent, not escape or explanation.
- Pain = **delight (ānanda)** received by a consciousness too divided, weak or narrow to bear it
  rightly — always state the guard: this is not a denial that pain is experientially real.
- Objections: instrumentalises suffering · deferred justification · the Divine authored the
  Inconscient · the delight-claim denies suffering. Each has a reply and a residue.
- Verdict formula: **integrated** into the involution–evolution metaphysics, **not solved**.

### T. Criticisms, replies and the graded verdict

- Six standard: verifiability of descent · philosophy or spiritual autobiography · emergence from the
  Inconscient · arbitrariness of the planes · excess of teleology · spiritual hubris.
- Four further: **determinism** · **elitism** · **category confusion** · **unfalsifiability**.
- Always in the form **objection → best reply → residual force**.
- **Strong:** original reconciliation of non-dualism with this-worldly commitment · answers the
  world-negation charge · Supermind and poises give a coherent One→Many account · Integral Yoga is
  non-sectarian.
- **Weak:** involution premise load-bearing and near question-begging · descent not publicly
  verifiable · theodicy risks instrumentalising suffering · the scheme is elaborate and
  experience-dependent.
- **Contested:** exegesis or reconstruction · classification of the system · the status of bodily
  transformation.
- One-liners: 10 marks — world-affirming Vedānta, caveat on verification; 15 marks — powerful
  synthesis resting on a controversial premise; 20 marks — most original in reconciling Spirit with
  real multiplicity, yet premise, theodicy and descent remain contested.

### U. Debates, comparisons, PYQ routing and architecture

- **Indian:** Śaṃkara (non-duality kept, illusionism refused; Supermind replaces illusion as
  mediation) · Patañjali and Sāṃkhya (dualism refused; **prakṛti** transformed) · Rāmānuja (shared
  anti-illusionism; God's body **eternally** vs a Divine **becoming**) · Buddhism (ego-critique
  accepted, cessation refused) · the Gītā (spirituality of action radicalised) · materialism
  (direction reversed) · religious mysticism (transcendence accepted, **return** insisted upon).
- **Western — illustrative only, always with the disanalogy, at most one or two lines, after the
  Aurobindo case:** Bergson (impersonal **élan vital**, no prior involution, no determinate goal) ·
  Hegel (dialectical and conceptual; no knowledge by identity, no yogic method, no descent) ·
  Teilhard (Christological and personalist; Omega at the end, no involution; parallel development,
  not influence) · Nietzsche (naturalistic, anti-metaphysical) · Whitehead (no planes, no descent) ·
  Plotinus (return **away from** matter).
- Writing "Aurobindo is the Indian Bergson/Hegel/Teilhard" without the disanalogy is a marked
  weakness.
- **PYQ routing:** 2018 Q8(c) 15 evolution + triple transformation + gnostic being · 2019 Q8(b) 15
  unique position of Supermind · 2020 Q8(a) 20 life divine as possibility and inevitability · 2021
  Q8(a) 20 psychic being as first step, explain and examine · 2022 Q8(b) 15 integral yoga in triple
  transformation, explain and evaluate · 2023 Q5(e) 10 nature and role of Supermind in evolution ·
  2024 Q7(c) 15 double movement, discuss · 2025 Q8(b) 15 ascetic and materialist partial in their
  negation.
- **Directives:** examine = expose presuppositions · critically examine = objection + reply +
  residue · evaluate = weighed conclusion · discuss a quotation = decode, locate, argue, judge.
- **Budgets:** 10 ≈ 150 words · 15 ≈ 250 · 20 ≈ 350.
- **Quotation discipline:** reproduce verified printed wording exactly; otherwise use attributed
  doctrinal formulations **without** quotation marks and never invent an edition or page.

### V. Optional Advanced depth — separately marked, never required

- Module 1 — Overmind as lower intensity or different principle (structural, not gradual).
- Module 2 — psychic being against individual eternal self and the classical witness.
- Module 3 — teleological evolution as interpretation, not alternative science.
- Module 4 — liberation against collective transformation as the system's centre.
- Deployment: one insight is usually enough, two is the ceiling, and it belongs in the final
  evaluative paragraph. **No core answer in this package depends on any of it.**

### W. Master trap list

1. Supermind is **not** a superior intellect.
2. Integral Yoga does **not** stop at individual liberation.
3. Aurobindo does **not** reject the Vedāntic Absolute — he rejects Śaṃkara's illusionism about the
   world.
4. Involution is **not** a dated historical event.
5. The psychic being (**caitya puruṣa**) is **not** the Sāṃkhya **puruṣa**.
6. Aurobindo's evolution is **not** Darwinian.
7. Life Divine does **not** mean heaven or post-mortem bliss.
8. Overmind is **not** the same as Supermind.
9. Aurobindo is **not** a simple neo-Vedāntin repetition of Advaita.
10. Matter is **not** anti-spiritual.
11. Knowledge by identity is **not** ordinary intuition.
12. Do **not** identify supramental knowledge with Patañjali's **ṛtambharā prajñā**.
13. Individual eternal self (**jīvātman**) and psychic being are **not** the same.
14. Evil is **real but not ultimate** — and the theodicy is **not** problem-free.
15. Western comparisons are **illustrative only**; always add the disanalogy.

```ascii-master
ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 8/22: Knowledge by identity and knowledge by separation

  > CAUTION: the exact enumeration and naming VARY. Present a GRADED SCHEME.

  GRADE 1 — KNOWLEDGE BY IDENTITY
    knower === known — no subject-object gap; Spirit knows itself directly,
    because consciousness and existence are NOT DIVIDED there
    NATIVE TO: Existence-Consciousness-Bliss and SUPERMIND
                              |  separation appears
  GRADE 2 — KNOWLEDGE BY INTIMATE DIRECT CONTACT
    knower --( enters )--> known — separation PRESENT but BRIDGED
    LEVEL: higher spiritual / intuitive contact
                              |  contact becomes external
  GRADE 3 — KNOWLEDGE BY SEPARATIVE DIRECT CONTACT
    knower --| gap |--> known — direct contact REMAINS, but from OUTSIDE
    LEVEL: sense-perception — direct, yet external
                              |  contact is replaced by signs
  GRADE 4 — WHOLLY SEPARATIVE INDIRECT KNOWLEDGE
    knower --> [ sign | symbol | image | inference | concept ] --> ?
    it REPRESENTS but does not POSSESS the thing
    LEVEL: discursive intellect and empirical representation
    <<< ORDINARY HUMAN KNOWING LIVES HERE >>>

  THE SEVEN-STEP ARGUMENT
    1. ordinary mind is CONSTITUTIVELY SEPARATIVE — it divides and sets subject
       against object
    2. therefore mind can never POSSESS the thing; only represent, classify,
       compare, infer, image
    3. mental knowledge is PARTIAL, MEDIATED and ERROR-LIABLE
    4. this epistemic limit is the CORRELATE of the metaphysical ignorance
       (avidyā) produced by involution
    5. SUPERMIND is truth-consciousness (vijñāna / ṛta-cit): it knows BY BEING
    6. transformation is therefore NOT more mental knowledge but a CHANGE IN THE
       MODE OF KNOWING
    7. hence Integral Yoga seeks a supramental DESCENT, not a mental ASCENT to
       refined concepts

  NOT MYSTICISM-AS-BLANK-INEFFABILITY
    knowledge by identity is SELF-LUMINOUS, DETERMINATE and COMPREHENSIVE:
    it knows the many AS the One and the One AS the many. Mind cannot: it either
    stresses unity by suppressing multiplicity, or stresses multiplicity by
    losing unity. => THIS is why Aurobindo can AFFIRM the world.

  +-------------------------+-----------------------------+---------------------+

  |                         | FORCE; the demand that      | consciousness, also |
  |                         | knowledge require duality   | faced by Advaita's  |
  |                         | is a MENTAL limitation      | svayaṃ-prakāśa      |
  | empiricist/positivist:  | yoga as a REPEATABLE INNER  | relocates rather    |

  |                         |                             | verification problem|
  | "truth-consciousness"   | the terms name MODES OF     | hard to translate   |
  | sounds metaphorical     | EXPERIENCE AND BEING inside | into analytic       |
  |                         | the systematic metaphysics  | criteria of truth   |
  +-------------------------+-----------------------------+---------------------+
```

```ascii-master
    matter RESTATES rather than explains the mystery, and involution is more
    economical IF evolution is directional. RESIDUE: a naturalist can deny
    directionality and treat consciousness as emergent.
```

#### ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 16/22: The double movement of ascent and descent

```ascii-master
ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 16/22: The double movement of ascent and descent

                              SUPERMIND
                                  ^   |
                    ASCENT        |   |        DESCENT
                                  |   v
                              OVERMIND
                                  ^   |
                              INTUITION
                                  ^   |

                                  ^   |

                                  ^   |

                        (the nature to be transformed)

  ASCENT — consciousness RISES from ordinary mentality through the higher planes
           order: Higher Mind -> Illumined Mind -> Intuition -> Overmind ->
           Supermind; FREES consciousness from narrow ego-bound mentality
  DESCENT — what is realised ABOVE must COME DOWN into the lower nature:
           peace, light, force, wideness, and finally SUPRAMENTAL TRUTH into
           MIND, LIFE and BODY.  ONLY THEN is transformation possible.

  +---------------------------------------------------------------------+
  |  THE TWO FAILURE MODES — this is the ARGUMENT, not decoration        |
  |  ASCENT ALONE .... may give liberation, illumination, transcendence  |
  |                    BUT can leave the LOWER NATURE UNTRANSFORMED      |
  |  DESCENT ALONE ... IMPOSSIBLE — higher consciousness must first be   |
  |                    CONTACTED before it can be brought down           |
  |  => THEREFORE transformation REQUIRES BOTH                           |
  +---------------------------------------------------------------------+

  WHO ACTS? the practitioner PREPARES THE INSTRUMENT (aspiration, rejection,
  surrender); the DESCENT is MEDIATED BY THE DIVINE ŚAKTI. Presenting descent as
  something the practitioner ACHIEVES converts Integral Yoga into VOLUNTARISM.

  RECURS THROUGH THE TRIPLE TRANSFORMATION
    psychic ...... an inward-and-forward turn permitting the FIRST DESCENTS
    spiritual .... the DESCENT of the planes contacted in ascent
    supramental .. the DESCENT of the Supermind itself — hence decisive

  VERIFIED 2024 WORDING (retain exactly, invent no citation):
    "Our Yoga is a double movement of ascent and descent."

  EVALUATIVE NOTE: the FIRST failure mode — that transcendence can leave ordinary
  nature unchanged — is broadly recognisable in the history of mysticism. The
  SECOND half — that a descent of this kind transforms the BODY — is the
  contested claim. Distinguish them; "discuss" rewards exactly that.
```

```ascii-master
ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 19/22: Divine life as possibility and inevitability, and evil

  QUESTION 1 — IS DIVINE LIFE POSSIBLE?   [2020 Q8(a), 20 marks]  FIVE REASONS
    1. Matter is INVOLVED SPIRIT, not an alien anti-spiritual substance
    2. life and mind have ALREADY EMERGED from apparently inconscient Matter
       << the STRONGEST reason: the OPPONENT ALREADY CONCEDES IT >>
    3. SUPERMIND coordinates unity and multiplicity => not foreign to manifestation
    4. psychic and spiritual transformation PREPARE mind, life and body
    5. the GNOSTIC BEING names the individual form of the result

  QUESTION 2 — IS IT INEVITABLE?
    YES, as the INNER DIRECTION of cosmic evolution: mind is TRANSITIONAL and the
    involved truth-consciousness PRESSES toward fuller manifestation
    NO, it does NOT mean:  x every present individual must become supramental
                           x history follows a FIXED TIMETABLE
                           x human effort MECHANICALLY produces the result
    BLOCKS AUTOMATISM: the scheme requires ASPIRATION/CALL FROM BELOW *and*
    SANCTION/DESCENT FROM ABOVE; openness, preparation and surrender remain
    significant.
    CALIBRATION LINE: cosmic teleology is STRONGER than mere possibility but
    WEAKER than empirical prediction with a specified date and mechanism.
    COLLECTIVE: the spread of a NEW PRINCIPLE OF CONSCIOUSNESS through terrestrial
    life — NOT rule by a self-certified spiritual elite.
    STATUS: SPECULATIVE; defended by coherence, prior emergence and disciplined
    inner evidence.

  THE PROBLEM OF EVIL — WHY IT IS SHARPER HERE
    EXIT 1 CLOSED: the Advaitic escape (evil belongs to the everyday level,
    vyāvahārika, and is finally sublated) is unavailable — the world is REAL
    MANIFESTATION.
    EXIT 2 CLOSED: the FREE-WILL DEFENCE is unavailable — suffering rooted in the
    INCONSCIENT PRECEDES human moral choice.
    => a direct PAPER-II bridge (Philosophy of Religion, Problem of Evil)

  THE FIVE-STEP ANSWER
    1. evil, falsehood, suffering, death = consequences of INCONSCIENCE produced
       by involution
    2. division -> ego, desire, conflict, error, pain => evil is REAL IN
       MANIFESTATION but NOT ORIGINAL in Existence-Consciousness-Bliss
    3. evil is REAL BUT NOT ULTIMATE — a STAGE of manifestation
    4. evil is INSTRUMENTAL and TRANSITIONAL — the Inconscient's resistance makes
       the drama of emergence possible
    5. therefore evil is TRANSFORMED by supramental descent, not explained away
       or escaped

  THE PAIN / DELIGHT CLAIM — always with its guard
    all experience is a modified form of the DELIGHT OF EXISTENCE; PAIN is ĀNANDA
    received by a consciousness TOO DIVIDED, WEAK OR NARROW to bear it rightly.
    GUARD: NOT a denial that pain is experientially real — a metaphysical claim
    about DISTORTED CONTACT under division. FIT: Ānanda is ultimate; the
    Inconscient is self-oblivion, not a second anti-divine principle.

  SAFE VERDICT: STRONG in linking evil to ignorance and making TRANSFORMATION the
  answer; WEAK where the cost of cosmic evolution is borne by SUFFERING
  INDIVIDUALS. NEVER write "fully solved"; write INTEGRATED into the
  involution-evolution metaphysics.
```

#### ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 20/22: The critical ledger and the graded verdict

```ascii-master
ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 20/22: The critical ledger and the graded verdict

  SET A — SIX STANDARD CRITICISMS   (objection -> reply -> residue)
   1. VERIFIABILITY OF THE DESCENT
      OBJ no public empirical evidence for descent or a gnostic species
      RPL the supramental is an evolutionary POSSIBILITY not exhausted by present
          empirical conditions; mind could not have been "proved" from the
          standpoint of mere matter BEFORE it emerged
      RES blocks PREMATURE CLOSURE; does NOT prove the doctrine
   2. PHILOSOPHY OR SPIRITUAL AUTOBIOGRAPHY?
      OBJ planes and supramental claims rely on personal yogic experience
      RPL much of Indian philosophy accepts yogic perception (yogic pratyakṣa) as
          a genuine mode of insight; and the system may be assessed by COHERENCE,
          EXPLANATORY POWER and INTEGRATIVE RANGE
      RES discussable, though not experimentally public
   3. EMERGENCE FROM THE INCONSCIENT
      OBJ if Matter is truly inconscient, emergence is unintelligible
      RPL the Inconscient is consciousness HIDDEN, not ABSENT => emergence is
          MANIFESTATION, not production from sheer non-being
      RES the phrase stays paradoxical and must be EXPLAINED
   4. ARE THE PLANES ARBITRARY?
      OBJ why THESE planes, in THIS order?
      RPL derived from SYSTEMATIC SPIRITUAL EXPERIENCE; the order reflects
          distinct experienced modes; the hierarchy BRIDGES otherwise
          disconnected levels
      RES the warrant remains INTERNAL
   5. DOES TELEOLOGY OVERSTATE COSMIC PURPOSE?
      OBJ modern science generally avoids intrinsic cosmic purpose
      RPL not competing with proximate description; a METAPHYSICAL
          INTERPRETATION of emergence
      RES risks UNFALSIFIABILITY if insulated from every challenge
   6. IS WORLD-TRANSFORMATION HUBRISTIC?
      OBJ classical traditions stress humility before embodied limits
      RPL transformation is NOT egoic self-assertion; it is grounded in SURRENDER
          to the Divine Force, not human voluntarism
      RES the ambition remains large; the reply is doctrinally solid

  SET B — FOUR FURTHER OBJECTIONS
   DETERMINISM ......... OBJ if Supermind is inevitable, freedom and Yoga are
                         redundant. RPL cosmic direction does not fix each
                         individual's timetable or response; aspiration,
                         rejection and surrender are modes of PARTICIPATION.
                         RES inevitability vs genuine refusal remains difficult.
   ELITISM ............. OBJ a "gnostic race" could legitimate hierarchy or
                         domination. RPL gnostic consciousness is defined by
                         EGO-TRANSCENDENCE, UNITY and SERVICE, not privilege; no
                         one may certify superiority by assertion.
                         RES private experience gives WEAK PUBLIC SAFEGUARDS
                         against charismatic abuse.
   CATEGORY CONFUSION .. OBJ biology, psychology and metaphysics treated as ONE
                         process. RPL levels SUPPLEMENT, not replace.
                         RES transitions between levels still need ARGUMENT.
   UNFALSIFIABILITY .... OBJ every failure is explained as incomplete preparation
                         or delayed descent. RPL coherence, transformative
                         experience, cumulative phenomenology.
                         RES without shared criteria and possible DEFEATERS, the
                         claim risks INSULATION from criticism.

  THE GRADED VERDICT
   STRONG ..... original reconciliation of spiritual non-dualism with evolutionary,
                this-worldly commitment · answers the charge that Indian
                philosophy is world-negating · Supermind and its poises give a
                coherent One->Many account · Integral Yoga is NON-SECTARIAN
   WEAK ....... involution premise LOAD-BEARING, near question-begging against
                emergentism · supramental descent NOT publicly verifiable ·
                theodicy risks INSTRUMENTALISING SUFFERING · the scheme of planes,
                beings and transformations is ELABORATE and largely dependent on
                yogic experience
   CONTESTED .. whether his readings of Veda, Upaniṣads and Gītā are EXEGESIS or
                RECONSTRUCTION · whether the system is philosophy, yogic
                phenomenology, metaphysical theology or a synthesis · the status
                of claims about BODILY TRANSFORMATION

  ONE-LINE VERDICTS BY MARK-WEIGHT
   10 ... world-affirming Vedānta; caveat: the supramental future is hard to verify
   15 ... a powerful synthesis of Existence-Consciousness-Bliss, evolution and
          yoga, resting on the controversial premise that what evolves must first
          be involved
   20 ... most original in reconciling non-dual Spirit with real multiplicity and
          terrestrial transformation; yet premise, theodicy and descent remain
          philosophically contested
```

#### ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 21/22: Debates and the controlled Western comparison

```ascii-master
ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 21/22: Debates and the controlled Western comparison

  INDIAN POSITIONING — the comparative spine
   ŚAṂKARA ...... Brahman / world as illusion / mediation by māyā-avidyā /
                  liberation by knowledge / sublation
                  VS Existence-Consc.-Bliss / REAL manifestation / mediation by
                  SUPERMIND / liberation PLUS transformation / divinisation
                  DEBATE POINT: preserves NON-DUALITY while rejecting ILLUSIONISM
   PATAÑJALI .... puruṣa-prakṛti dualism / kaivalya / restraint, cessation,
                  isolation / world = bondage-field
                  VS dynamic spiritual monism / integral transformation / ascent
                  AND descent / world = transformation-field
   SĀṂKHYA ...... two ultimate realities / independent prakṛti / liberation by
                  discrimination / prakṛti evolves FOR puruṣa's release
                  VS one Divine reality in graded manifestation / matter =
                  concealed Spirit / transformation of nature by Spirit /
                  evolution = the Divine's self-manifestation
   RĀMĀNUJA ..... world = REAL BODY OF GOD eternally / individual = real dependent
                  mode / loving service and communion
                  VS world = real manifestation and BECOMING / individual = real
                  centre with an EVOLUTIONARY DESTINY / divine life by supramental
                  transformation
                  BOTH reject illusionism and affirm world + individual;
                  DIFFERENCE: God's body ETERNALLY vs a Divine BECOMING with a
                  SUPRAMENTAL FUTURE
   BUDDHISM ..... denial of a permanent surface self / nirvāṇa, cessation of
                  ignorance and craving / often read as release from suffering
                  VS denies EGO but affirms Self, individual eternal self and
                  psychic being / transformation of life by supramental descent /
                  world = field for divine manifestation
                  ACCEPTS the ego-critique; REJECTS nirvāṇa as final IF it means
                  cessation or world-negation
   THE GĪTĀ ..... major scriptural warrant for spirituality IN ACTION; its
                  synthesis of jñāna, bhakti and karma supports the claim that
                  yoga need not be life-renouncing; action without egoic desire
                  becomes part of the transformation of nature
                  SAFE LINE: he RADICALISES the Gītā's spirituality of action into
                  an evolutionary yoga of terrestrial divinisation
   MATERIALISM .. consciousness as a derivative accident of matter
                  VS matter as a derivative poise of CONCEALED CONSCIOUSNESS
                  => a sustained CRITIQUE OF REDUCTIONISM
   MYSTICISM .... often culminates in transcendence BEYOND world and action
                  VS transcendence ACCEPTED but RETURN, DESCENT and TERRESTRIAL
                  MANIFESTATION insisted upon => evolutionary, this-world-affirming

  CONTROLLED WESTERN COMPARISON — one or two lines MAXIMUM, ALWAYS with the
  disanalogy, ALWAYS AFTER the Aurobindo case is complete
   BERGSON ...... CONTACT: rejects mechanistic and purely Darwinian accounts;
                  evolution creative and directional; intuition over analytic
                  intellect.
                  DISANALOGY: élan vital is an IMPERSONAL life-impulse with NO
                  prior involution and NO determinate goal; its creativity is
                  OPEN-ENDED, whereas Aurobindo's evolution is TELEOLOGICALLY
                  CLOSED by the recovery of Existence-Consciousness-Bliss;
                  Bergson's intuition is DURATION-CONSCIOUSNESS, not a supramental
                  plane; no divine descent, no bodily transformation.
                  DO NOT ASSERT HISTORICAL INFLUENCE.
   HEGEL ........ CONTACT: dynamic Absolutes; the finite is NOT mere illusion but
                  a moment of the Infinite's self-realisation.
                  DISANALOGY: Hegel's process is DIALECTICAL and CONCEPTUAL,
                  through contradiction and sublation (Aufhebung), toward ABSOLUTE
                  KNOWING as self-conscious THOUGHT; Aurobindo's is SUPRAMENTAL
                  and EXPERIENTIAL, aiming at divine life in matter and body;
                  Hegel has no knowledge by identity, no yogic method, no descent,
                  and would treat bodily divinisation as a CATEGORY ERROR.
   TEILHARD ..... CONTACT: the CLOSEST structural parallel — cosmogenesis,
                  complexity-consciousness, noosphere, Omega Point; both affirm
                  matter and convergence.
                  DISANALOGY: Teilhard's framework is CHRISTOLOGICAL and
                  PERSONALIST; Omega is the cosmic Christ and convergence occurs
                  at the END of the world-process; Aurobindo's Supermind DESCENDS
                  INTO and TRANSFORMS terrestrial life INCLUDING THE BODY, WITHIN
                  manifestation; Teilhard has no involution as prior
                  self-concealment and no yogic method. Same-period convergence =
                  PARALLEL DEVELOPMENT, not influence.
   NIETZSCHE .... CONTACT: the human as TRANSITIONAL; self-overcoming beyond the
                  present human type.
                  DISANALOGY: the Übermensch is NATURALISTIC, ANTI-METAPHYSICAL
                  and centred on will and earth WITHOUT Divine descent;
                  Aurobindo's supramental being arises FROM Truth-Consciousness.
   WHITEHEAD .... CONTACT: process-friendly; rejects a static, world-negating
                  metaphysics.
                  DISANALOGY: God and actual occasions do NOT map onto planes of
                  consciousness, involution, supramental descent or bodily
                  transformation.
   PLOTINUS ..... CONTACT: procession from the One and return to it resemble
                  involution and evolution.
                  DISANALOGY: the Plotinian RETURN is an ASCENT AWAY FROM MATTER,
                  the dimmest emanation; Aurobindo's completion is DESCENT INTO
                  Matter and its TRANSFORMATION.

  RUBRIC: illustrative only · never a substitute for Aurobindo's own argument ·
  always with the disanalogy · at most one or two lines · writing "Aurobindo is
  the Indian Bergson/Hegel/Teilhard" WITHOUT the disanalogy is a MARKED WEAKNESS.
```

```ascii-master
   FREQUENCIES: Supermind x2 · triple transformation x2 · divine life · psychic
   being · double movement · ascetic-materialist. INVOLUTION: never asked alone.

  DIRECTIVE DECODER
   DISCUSS ............ define -> stages -> significance -> ONE contrast
                        close: "Thus the doctrine is central because..."
   EXAMINE ............ claim -> EXPOSE ASSUMPTIONS -> test with objection/reply
                        close: "It is coherent if its presuppositions are granted."
   CRITICALLY EXAMINE . exposition -> STRONGEST objection -> reply -> RESIDUAL
                        WEAKNESS
                        close: "Original but not free from the verification/
                        presupposition problem."
   ANALYSE ............ decompose: Existence-Consc.-Bliss, Supermind, involution,
                        evolution, yoga
                        close: "The parts form an integral metaphysical chain."
   EVALUATE ........... strengths + weaknesses, WEIGHED conclusion
                        close: "Strong as synthesis; weak where future descent is
                        unverifiable."
   COMPARE ............ table + at least one DECISIVE DISANALOGY
   DISTINGUISH ........ define each separately -> contrast line -> payoff
   ELUCIDATE .......... formula -> doctrine -> example -> implication
   COMMENT ............ explain -> locate in system -> evaluate
   BRING OUT .......... doctrine -> metaphysical, yogic and ethical implications
   DO YOU AGREE? ...... partial agreement -> qualification -> calibrated judgement
   QUOTED LINE ........ decode WITHOUT inventing a citation -> explain terms ->
                        link to the system -> examine presupposition/objection

  ANSWER ARCHITECTURE
   10 MARKS (~150 words)  L1 define the exact term · L2-4 doctrinal structure in
                          2-3 technical points · L5-6 the key contrast (Advaita /
                          Darwin / Patañjali) · L7 one evaluative line
   15 MARKS (~250 words)  Frame: the problem addressed · Body A: define precisely ·
                          Body B: inner logic or stages · Body C: contrast with the
                          nearest rival · Assess: one criticism + one reply ·
                          Close: Life Divine / transformation
   20 MARKS (~350 words)  Frame: locate Aurobindo, identify the issue · Body A:
                          full exposition · Body B: connect to involution and
                          Integral Yoga · Body C: contrast with Śaṃkara, Darwin or
                          Patañjali · Assess: criticism and reply · Close: the
                          significance of transformation of life on earth

  MICRO-SKELETONS
   SUPERMIND'S UNIQUE POSITION  define Truth-Consciousness / Real-Idea -> locate
                                between the Absolute and lower creation -> unity-
                                in-multiplicity avoids illusion AND leap -> NOT
                                the Absolute, NOT Mind -> close on originality
   PSYCHIC BEING                define the evolving soul-entity -> first awakening
                                reorients toward the Divine -> enables psychic ->
                                spiritual -> supramental -> NOT Sāṃkhya puruṣa,
                                NOT the ego -> first indispensable step
   DOUBLE MOVEMENT              ascent ladder -> descent into mind-life-body ->
                                ascent without descent = liberation not
                                transformation -> descent without ascent
                                impossible -> both define Integral Yoga
   ASCETIC AND MATERIALIST      ascetic affirms Spirit denies Matter ->
                                materialist the reverse -> Spirit is the truth of
                                Matter, Matter the field of Spirit -> neither
                                renunciation nor reductionism is final -> Life
                                Divine on earth

  QUOTATION DISCIPLINE: reproduce VERIFIED PRINTED wording exactly (2024 and 2025
  statements); otherwise use ATTRIBUTED DOCTRINAL FORMULATIONS WITHOUT quotation
  marks — "all life is Yoga" (Synthesis of Yoga) · "man is a transitional being;
  he is not final" (Life Divine) · "nothing evolves from Matter unless its power
  was already involved there" (exam-safe paraphrase) · "Supermind is truth-
  consciousness that holds unity and multiplicity by knowledge by identity"
  (Life Divine). NEVER invent an edition or page reference.

  CLOSING SPINE FOR ANY AUROBINDO ANSWER
   involution makes evolution possible -> Supermind makes manifestation
   intelligible -> the psychic being makes transformation personal -> the triple
   transformation makes it structured -> Integral Yoga makes it conscious -> the
   gnostic being and the Life Divine make it terrestrial.
   QUALIFIED CONCLUSION: an ambitious world-affirming synthesis that answers the
   charge of world-negation more completely than any other modern Indian system,
   and whose open question is not its world-affirmation but its WARRANT.
```
